# Lifeweaver's sounds: <sound id> = <file under E:\Overwatch Archive\extracted\154088\> (build 154088). Same format as
# mercy.txt / jetpack_cat.txt; build_asset_pack.py sounds copies / mixes them into the local pack. Nothing here has been
# listened to. Read 2026-10-29 offline (Overwatch and Battle.net checked closed before every archive read and DataTool
# run): his 02C sounds' Wwise banks decoded exactly (ww.py, dump_banks.py, emote_events.py / wwfull.py from
# research\emote-sounds\scripts: children from each object's parent id, random / sequence / switch / layer parameters,
# volume, make-up gain, pitch, delays, the events' actions, RTPC curves), the Init bank's bus volumes and game parameter
# defaults (1E818, stmg.py). Scripts: E:\Overwatch Archive\research\lifeweaver\scripts\sounds\ (lw_gen.py writes this
# file); their data (decode, bank dump, measurements, the timed effect / clip reads fx_timed.json, contact sets, voice
# lines, the RTPC defaults): research\lifeweaver\sounds\. Files: his skin and voice extraction are in
# research\lifeweaver\extract, not under the build root, so those paths reach it through ../../research/lifeweaver/
# extract/ (lw_gen.py --canonical writes Heroes/Lifeweaver/... and HeroVoice/Lifeweaver/... for when that extraction is put
# under extracted\154088); what it lacked was extracted for this map into ExtraSounds\Lifeweaver (manifest.json there:
# the blossom's and the thorn's projectile sounds, Superbloom, footsteps); the thorns' contact set is Mercy's blaster's
# (ExtraSounds\Mercy\HitBlock / HitCharacter). Ids: 02C sounds (9682 = 000000009682.02C), 00D effects, 006 clips, 08F
# clip animation effects, 049 contact sets; graphs 01B with their node numbers. Confidence: H read in the data, M
# inferred, L guess.
# Gains: Wwise volume + make-up gain summed from each file up through its containers and mixers (the middle of a random
# range included) plus its output bus chain, plus RTPC curves (dB scaling 20*log10(1+y); linear between points) and
# SetVolume actions. RTPCs are read at their game parameter's default (the Init bank's list: 0 for most, 100 for the
# fire rate 4066808683, 1 for 559517101) unless a rule says (Mercy's map read them all at 0); modulators: an LFO at its
# middle, a time modulator at its start on one-shots and at its end on loops. As in mercy.txt, ONE offset per family
# keeps his sounds' balance with each other, bringing the family's loudest single layer to -1 dBFS (the builder's
# limiter catches sums): own +3 dB (loudest: lifeweaver.superbloom), 3p +5.5 dB (loudest: lifeweaver.tree.death).
# Pitch: cents as a speed factor (*). Low / high-pass filters, random pitch / volume per play, fades, Seek (random start
# points), Wwise states (the -20 dB / lpf 25 'muffled' state on most of his mixers, group 177926770) and the voice
# priority RTPCs are not reproduced. Layers more than 60 dB down are left out, and in loops those 20 dB under the
# loudest loop. A loop's LOOP LENGTH is its longest bed within 6 dB of the loudest layer (Mercy's rule); shorter layers
# are tiled across it and beds more than 15 % longer are left out.
# Own vs others: every one of his STUSounds has audience 2 but 5A35 (1). Each effect holds one copy with no range
# (m_ACB34EFC -1 or 0), read as the player's own (M, Mercy's reading; their mixers confirm it: they sit under the 2D bus
# 'sfx_2d', its 'abilities_1p' / 'weapon_1p' / 'foley_1p' children, or a 2D feedback branch, while the ranged copies
# sit under '3p' / '3d': bus names recovered by hashing, FNV-1 of 2d, 3d, 1p, 3p, sfx_2d, abilities_1p / _3p,
# weapon_1p / _3p, foley_1p / _3p, impacts_1p / _3p, lfe), and one or two ranged copies for everyone else; where there
# are two their STU fields are identical (read as the allies' and the enemies' copy, L which): the louder or the one
# sharing his own copy's file is built as _3p, a different-file twin as an EXTRA '_enemy' rule. Platform and tree
# sounds with no own copy are built once for everyone ('both'). 'own' is the player the effect plays on: his, or the
# gripped / healed ally's where a rule says. 'lfe' layers are sub-bass thuds (all their energy under 300 Hz) on the
# lfe bus, kept as Mercy's, Lucio's and Jetpack Cat's maps keep them.

# ===== How each game event is put together (rules)
# lifeweaver.blossom.charge.start: charging start, own: the one-shot layer of 02C 9682 (30E1's no-range copy, on the
#  weapon_1p bus: his, M): a random 3 of 1.34-1.40 s.
# Trigger: Healing Blossom's charge starts (primary held, a blossom in the clip, which it spends): 21C6
#  StateChaseVar#719 (the charge v1604 -> 1 over 1.15 s) holds 30DE on hp 000A (#724, no sound) and 30E1 (#725);
#  30E1's 02C 9682 Play event's one-shot layer.
# Layers: a 9682 426067983 RanSeq 1.34-1.40 s x3, net -10.5.
@lifeweaver.blossom.charge.start = lifeweaver.blossom.charge.start.a:-7.5
# lifeweaver.blossom.charge.loop: charging loop, own: 9682's two endless Sound loops (6.22 s +80 cents, its Play 0.3 s
#  late with a 200 ms fade-in; 7.25 s +125 cents from a random start point, an LFO on its level read at its middle).
#  Neither loop follows the charge (no charge RTPC on them, H). 9682's Play also targets object 970972222, in none of
#  the build's 1148 banks (H: nothing).
# Trigger: while charging and while held at full: #719 holds 30E1 (#725) until the release (with or without a target),
#  a reload or death.
# Layers: a 9682 429815542 Sound 7.25 s +125 cents, net -8.5, loops every 6.746 s; b 9682 192474110 Sound 6.22 s +80
#  cents, net -7.5, loops every 5.938 s.
# LOOP LENGTH 6.746 s: play it again every LOOP LENGTH (the built file runs 6.75 s: shorter layers are tiled to fill
# the loop and the last tile runs over; Overwatch's layers loop on their own lengths, which one file cannot do).
@lifeweaver.blossom.charge.loop = lifeweaver.blossom.charge.loop.a*1.075:-5.5 + lifeweaver.blossom.charge.loop.b*1.047@0.3:-4.5
# lifeweaver.blossom.charge.end: charge end, own: 9682's Stop event: the loops stop (1 s / 100 ms fades) and a random
#  3 of 0.70-0.80 s plays, pitched and levelled by the charge (RTPC 316118616 = df 09BD, 21C6 #1012's v1604: +630
#  cents and -10 dB at 0, 0 / 0 dB at 1); its 1 s SetPitch glide on the 6.2 s loop is not reproduced. Built at charge
#  1.0 (above 0.9).
# Trigger: the charge ends (the release with or without a target, a reload): 30E1 stops, which runs 9682's Stop event;
#  pick the bin of the charge at the release (#954's own bins: c <= 0.3, <= 0.9, above).
# Layers: a 9682 Stop-event 709259821 RanSeq 0.70-0.80 s x3, net -6.5.
@lifeweaver.blossom.charge.end = lifeweaver.blossom.charge.end.a:-3.5

# ===== Voice (HeroVoice/Lifeweaver): the voice set 0227.05F's lines by moment (research\lifeweaver\sounds\
# voice_lines.json, from lw_voice.py: each line's 0B2, stimulus, weight and decoded criteria; files from DataTool's
# extract-hero-voice --subtitles-with-sounds, in research\lifeweaver\extract). His lines are spoken 0B2 files (each line's
# external 02C 16F3 is the shared dialogue slot, not a sound of its own). w = the line's weight (the builder turns it
# into Minecraft weights). Lines with criteria are left out, as Mercy's, Lucio's and Jetpack Cat's are (hero
# interactions, skins, scripted moments: counted per moment), except 'NOT ScriptedEvent 191E', which every effort sound
# carries and is treated as always true (kiriko-voice.md). Stimulus numbers (the .078): priority, radius m, volume dB,
# interrupt, cooldown s (m_A73F2EBE-m_DD27AC95 read as min-max, M); Voice.java's table takes priority, cooldown and
